# Primary-source clue queue — cross-book retrieval, not a theory verdict

- Record role: `RESEARCH / EVIDENCE ROUTER`
- Authority: `ADVISORY / NONE`; owner of **which source junctions to investigate next**, not owner of story outcomes
- Status: `EXPANDING — EARLY CHAPTER INTAKE`
- Updated: 2026-10-15
- Source and epistemic standard: [SOURCES.md](../SOURCES.md); reading coverage: [tracker](../Source/Main-Series/CLOSE-READING-STATUS.md)

**Interpretation rule:** A detail is not a clue just because it is unusual. Preserve narrative context and at least one ordinary explanation; distinguish directly observed action from a character's private inference, folk history or external fan idea. `TEST` means investigate, not accept.

| ID | Primary locator | Signal to revisit | Smallest useful test / rival explanation |

| C-097 | [AGOT 26](../Source/Main-Series/01-A-Game-of-Thrones/chapters/26-jon.md) | Sam reports blue-lipped Qartheen warlocks and a bull-blood ritual that did not instill courage | Do not conclude a failed ritual proves all warlock magic ineffective; the report is secondhand within Jon POV |
| C-098 | [AGOT 26](../Source/Main-Series/01-A-Game-of-Thrones/chapters/26-jon.md) | Jon dreams of empty Winterfell and compulsory descent into dark Stark crypts | Test later dreams and crypt geography; identity anxiety offers a strong nonsupernatural explanation |
| C-099 | [AGOT 26](../Source/Main-Series/01-A-Game-of-Thrones/chapters/26-jon.md) | Searches for Benjen find blazes ending abruptly in northwestern highlands | An actual last-trace detail, not proof of a particular captor or supernatural transformation |
| C-100 | [AGOT 26](../Source/Main-Series/01-A-Game-of-Thrones/chapters/26-jon.md) | Jon builds coalition for Sam but threatens Rast with Ghost to enforce protection | Track later leadership methods and retaliatory risk; kindness and coercion coexist |
| C-101 | [AGOT 27](../Source/Main-Series/01-A-Game-of-Thrones/chapters/27-eddard.md) | Ned adds fifty gold cloaks but transfers twenty Stark guards into Slynt's command | Preserve command and manpower accounting when reconstructing court violence |
| C-102 | [AGOT 27](../Source/Main-Series/01-A-Game-of-Thrones/chapters/27-eddard.md) | Jon Arryn and Stannis visited a brothel and separately investigated Tobho Mott's boy | First account comes through a former stablehand; second confirmed by Mott and Gendry; weigh independently |

| C-106 | [AGOT 27](../Source/Main-Series/01-A-Game-of-Thrones/chapters/27-eddard.md) | Unnamed red-brown-bearded man in purple pays Gendry's apprentice fee twice over | Trace physical description, payment and access; assigning a specific spymaster is presently THEORY |
| C-107 | [AGOT 27](../Source/Main-Series/01-A-Game-of-Thrones/chapters/27-eddard.md) | Gendry makes his own bull-headed helm and refuses to sell it | Material identifier and self-determination; do not assign heraldic destiny without later evidence |

| C-111 | [AGOT 28](../Source/Main-Series/01-A-Game-of-Thrones/chapters/28-catelyn.md) | Catelyn accuses and calls for Tyrion's arrest using a one-witness knife provenance | Distinguish eyewitness assault against Bran from accusation against Tyrion and downstream war effects |

## Priority rule

1. Record chronological cause/effect and major scene action before promoting any hidden-clue theory.
2. Prioritize a clue when it materially changes a character's plausible choices or can disconfirm multiple competing hypotheses.
3. Retain disputed details and discarded hypotheses with specific locators; avoid endless symbolism without a causal test.
4. When confirmed by a later chapter, update the canonical [character sheet](characters/README.md) or [theory case](theories/README.md), then update this index's reading priority.

This queue is **not** a ranked list of true fan theories or a completed series-wide clue census. It currently draws on the two hundred and fifty-one first-pass chapters listed in the coverage tracker.
